# Agents · I · part 5

1,068 entries, implementer to implementer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **implementer**: Builds one approved plan unit test-first, or fixes blocking review findings. It is the only role that runs specs and lint: focused tests as it works, then the full spec and lint commands **once at the end of its round**, recorded in its report. ([ali-sheiba/development-workflow](https://github.com/ali-sheiba/development-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Use to implement one approved plan unit test-first, or to fix blocking review findings. Runs focused tests as it works and the full specs and lint once at the end of its round. Works only on a non-protected branch. Does not commit. Stops after its single unit. ([ali-sheiba/development-workflow](https://github.com/ali-sheiba/development-workflow/blob/HEAD/adapters/claude-code/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Implements a change from a spec and an explorer brief, test-first, then fixes reviewer findings and commits when told the review passed. Use as the implement step of the explore-implement-review pipeline. ([aligneddev/BikeCommuteTracker](https://github.com/aligneddev/BikeCommuteTracker/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Builds one wave of work from a self-contained brief - reads the state files it is pointed at, edits only the files it owns, runs the project check before returning. Use for any implementation or fix handed off by the main agent. ([Aliolabadiena/sonelle](https://github.com/Aliolabadiena/sonelle/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **implementer**: Use when you need to implement a todo item from a plan. Receives item text and plan context. Writes code, writes tests, commits per cycle. ([aliyenidede/rea](https://github.com/aliyenidede/rea/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer.md) · ★1 · 3 copies)
- **implementer**: Turns a card with already-written, red acceptance tests into the smallest correct production code. Only writes production code; the guard denies writes to tests. ([alizici/claude-code-guardrails](https://github.com/alizici/claude-code-guardrails/blob/HEAD/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Implements one scoped task from the GitHub Project board end-to-end in a disjoint file set — code, migrations, tests, docs — and leaves the repo passing `pnpm verify`. Default cheap leaf worker via DeepSeek Flash. Give it the Task ID, exact owned/forbidden paths, and acceptance criteria. ([alliecatowo/patches](https://github.com/alliecatowo/patches/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **implementer**: Works one step of one task in a devteam project — writes the code, runs the step's verification command, commits, and reports. Dispatched by a supervisor with the step dispatch template. Do not use for anything else. ([alternative-intelligence-cp/claude-skills](https://github.com/alternative-intelligence-cp/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/devteam/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: You are implementing one phase (or a slice of one) from an already-reviewed task checklist. You did not write this plan and shouldn't second-guess its scope — your job is to execute the specific checklist items you've been given, correctly and in order, not to redesign the feature. ([ambreperson/live-coding-harness-devfest-afrique-francophone](https://github.com/ambreperson/live-coding-harness-devfest-afrique-francophone/blob/HEAD/.claude/skills/sdlc-implement/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Escreve o código de produção seguindo o tasks.md aprovado em docs/specs/&lt;feature&gt;/ até os testes do test-writer passarem. Não edita testes nem internal/tracker/ (isso é do tracker-specialist). Use depois que o plano foi aprovado. ([angelo848/arcadia](https://github.com/angelo848/arcadia/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Makes tests pass. Focus only on implementation, no extras. ([AniketAslaliya/debateFloor](https://github.com/AniketAslaliya/debateFloor/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer.md) · ★1 · 5 copies)
- **implementer**: Implements a bounded, approved assignment using repository-native patterns and stack rules, including focused tests and self-review, while preserving unrelated work. ([anjotadena/asterweave](https://github.com/anjotadena/asterweave/blob/HEAD/plugins/asterweave/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Implements a bounded, approved assignment using repository-native patterns. ([anjotadena/asterweave](https://github.com/anjotadena/asterweave/blob/HEAD/website/docs/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Use this agent for a single-writer arbeitsplan phase that implements one scoped change against a fixed contract — one writer, one worktree, one diff returned as data. Refuses fan-out (N writers over one scope is best-of-n with candidate-builder) and refuses to widen writeScope. Hands its diff to the landing step; behaviour-preserving restructuring goes to refactorer, removals to cleaner. ([Anselmoo/werkstoff](https://github.com/Anselmoo/werkstoff/blob/HEAD/plugins/arbeitsplan/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Реализует порученную задачу и проверяет результат. ([asaf1n/AI-Orchestrated-Workflow](https://github.com/asaf1n/AI-Orchestrated-Workflow/blob/HEAD/examples/antigravity/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Выполняет порученную задачу. ([asaf1n/AI-Orchestrated-Workflow](https://github.com/asaf1n/AI-Orchestrated-Workflow/blob/HEAD/examples/claude/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Builds one course sprint issue, or fixes one sent back, in its lane's worktree. Used only by the sprint-lane skill, for /sprint-lane runs; never for interactive work, because its --file-lock hook refuses edits in the main checkout. ([astevegreen/blockchain-technology-course](https://github.com/astevegreen/blockchain-technology-course/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Builds one GitHub issue or fixes one pull request, with tests, in its own worktree. Only for night-shift and sprint-lane runs, never for interactive work. ([astevegreen/terminal-dusk](https://github.com/astevegreen/terminal-dusk/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Implements exactly one task card in its worktree, RED first, to a green dod_command. Use when a card is dispatched by the aidlc loop. ([Asun28/AIDLC](https://github.com/Asun28/AIDLC/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **implementer**: Implement changes whose specification is already settled. Use for mechanical or well-scoped edits where the approach is decided and little trial-and-error is expected. Can batch several small related tasks in one run. ([atman-33/atman-marketplace](https://github.com/atman-33/atman-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/engineering/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Implements production code until a specified requirement's acceptance scenarios pass. Use for implementation PRs only, after the spec PR is merged. ([attila-balogh-biro86/atdd-orders](https://github.com/attila-balogh-biro86/atdd-orders/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Implementer, fixer or checkpoint runner for exactly one task of a controller-driven run. Use for every implementation, fix-round, continuation and checkpoint dispatch that names a brief file. ([BarabashkaD/dv-claude-dev-market](https://github.com/BarabashkaD/dv-claude-dev-market/blob/HEAD/plugins/dv-agent-controller/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Executes an already-written implementation plan step by step — writes the firmware code and CMocka tests, builds, runs them, reports what landed. Use after a plan exists. - **implementer**: TDD implementer for nectar build waves. Executes exactly one plan task — failing test first, minimal implementation, refactor — inside its declared file scope, and reports verified results with quoted test output. ([BEE-CODED/bee-dev](https://github.com/BEE-CODED/bee-dev/blob/HEAD/plugins/nectar/agents/implementer.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **implementer**: Full-stack TDD implementer for Bee tasks. Writes failing tests first, then minimal implementation, then refactors. ([BEE-CODED/bee-dev](https://github.com/BEE-CODED/bee-dev/blob/HEAD/plugins/bee/agents/implementer.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **implementer**: Builds one ticket (one task group of an OpenSpec change) in its own worktree and opens its PR with evidence. Use when the orchestrator dispatches a ticket, with the ticket id, change, group, worktree, branch and E2E_PORT. ([bendechrai/software-factory-workshop](https://github.com/bendechrai/software-factory-workshop/blob/HEAD/templates/act-9/.claude/agents/implementer.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **implementer**: Builds one ticket (one task group of an OpenSpec change) in its own worktree and opens its PR with evidence. Use when the orchestrator dispatches a ticket. ([bendechrai/software-factory-workshop](https://github.com/bendechrai/software-factory-workshop/blob/HEAD/templates/act-9/.agents/agents/implementer.md) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **implementer**: Implements a discrete unit of work from a plan. Writes code, runs tests, and returns a tight summary of what changed. Use this from an orchestrator when delegating implementation steps so the workflow rules below are applied automatically. ([BenjaminHolderbein/claude-orchestrate](https://github.com/BenjaminHolderbein/claude-orchestrate/blob/HEAD/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: TDD implementer — claims one task from the shared team task list, drives red-green-refactor, stages files. - **implementer**: 메인이 지정한 소유 파일 안에서만 코드·테스트를 수정하는 구현 담당 — 파일 소유권이 겹치지 않는 구현을 나눌 때 쓴다. git 작업·의존성 변경·마이그레이션 생성·운영 접근을 하지 않는다. ([bhnvx/ai_boilerplate](https://github.com/bhnvx/ai_boilerplate/blob/HEAD/claude/global/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)

- **implementer**: Builds one phase of a plan in a clean context and commits it on the plan's branch. Dispatch with the path of its brief. ([bmox0/dev-skills](https://github.com/bmox0/dev-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Executes a change whose approach is already decided. - **implementer**: Executes exactly one phase of an approved plan under the shared executor contract. ([bqp007/concord-agent-harness](https://github.com/bqp007/concord-agent-harness/blob/HEAD/adapters/claude-code/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Implements one scoped workit brief in the git worktree its lead made with `workit fanout worktree create`, and reports what changed and how it was checked. - **implementer**: Use this agent to implement production code that makes failing tests pass. This agent is part of the TDD orchestration system and runs as the third step in each phase. Test Writer → Writes failing tests 3. **Implementer (this agent)** → Makes tests pass, runs them, reports output 4. Validator → Verifies requirements met &lt;example&gt; Context: Phase-based TDD orchestration. orchestrator: "Phase 3 tests are failing as expected. - **implementer**: Implementa un plan aprobado ejecutándolo fase a fase. Lee el `improvement-plan.md` y el `tasks.md` de una iniciativa en `docs/roadmap/&lt;fecha&gt;-&lt;slug&gt;/`, escribe el código real del proyecto para cumplir cada tarea (T-XX) y sus criterios de aceptación, y mantiene `tasks.md` como **ledger canónico** marcando el estado de cada tarea (checkbox + estado) a medida que avanza. Trabaja sobre una rama, respeta los guardrails del repo y hace handoff a `qa` al terminar. Úsalo cuando el usuario diga "implementa el plan", "ejecuta las tareas", "desarrolla el roadmap", "implementa la fase X". ([daycry/custom-agents](https://github.com/daycry/custom-agents/blob/HEAD/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Implements a clearly specified, bounded coding task (a module, its tests, wiring, content entries) and proves it with the project's local checks. Use when the design is decided and the work is independent and large: spec'd edits across many files, or a long build you don't need to watch. Give it the goal, files, rules and acceptance criteria. Not when the task is small (a one-line fix), when each step needs the last one's result, or when it needs a design decision. Does not commit or push. - **implementer**: Implements features, writes tests, and commits code per the implementation plan. Escalates deviations — never deviates silently. Used by Team Dispatch. ([denormans/claude-code-workflow](https://github.com/denormans/claude-code-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Writes the code for one specific task of the plan. Invoke task by task, never with the whole plan at once.
